        <script type="application/ld+json">{"@type":"VideoObject","name":"BMH MoneyConfessions Ania","description":"No description","thumbnailUrl":"https://cfvod.kaltura.com/p/4699762/sp/469976200/thumbnail/entry_id/1_xbov2nx7/version/100011","uploadDate":"2026-06-30T10:34:00-04:00","contentUrl":"https://cdnapisec.kaltura.com/p/4699762/sp/469976200/playManifest/entryId/1_xbov2nx7/format/url/protocol/https","embedUrl":"https://cdnapisec.kaltura.com/p/4699762/sp/469976200/playManifest/entryId/1_xbov2nx7/format/url/protocol/https","width":1920,"height":1080,"duration":"PT2M20S","transcript":"[Music playing in the background] [Ania walking on set, sitting on chair.] On screen copy: Real people. Real confessions. Real guidance from Bank of America Better Money Habits® logo [Ania S. looking at camera and making a financial confession.] On screen copy: Ania S. Side Hustle Stylist Ania S.: Money is a tricky thing. There’s kind of a fear-based relationship there. You can work hard and earn a lot of it, but it can also be kind of taken away quickly from you. So … I have a confession. [Better Money Habits Champion appears on inset screen, bottom right corner.] I turned kind of a fun hobby into a side hustle and I totally let it get a little too out of control with one of my recent purchases. I purchased a $2,000 mink coat at a sample sale. I thought it's a high-ticket item. I can sell it for a lot. It was originally worth $10,000. So I'm at the register. The anxiety is building, the fear is building, the card swipes, and there's no going back from there. Literally over a year later, the coat is still in my closet. On screen copy: A Better Money Habits Champion on approaching a side hustle [Better Money Habits Champion Manny E. sitting at desk and looking at camera.] On screen copy: Manny E. Better Money Habits Champion Manny E.: The position Ania finds herself in is not uncommon. She's starting a small business, and in order for you to do that, you need a plan. You got to first start with researching the market. That way you're able to assess your risk, but also set boundaries for yourself on how much you're willing to put into your business without putting your own finances in jeopardy. In Ania's case, she came across a $10,000 coat that was marked down to 2,000. Her mind is screaming, \"Oh my gosh, that's a quick, easy $8,000 profit.\" That's an example of acting on emotion per se, based on your fear-based relationship with money. It's good to take a step back and really assess the risks, right? One of the things she can ask herself is, \"Are my finances good enough to be able to take a $2,000 hit?\" Determining ahead of time how much money you want to spend on your business will help you not make any rash decisions. No one's perfect when it comes to their finances. It's always better to have a plan and make modifications to it as things change than to not have a plan at all. On screen copy: Side Hustle Takeaways • A side hustle, no matter how small, is a business. • Create a business plan: research the market, assess the risks and opportunities. • Establish financial boundaries. • Make a plan to avoid rash or emotional decision making. Announcer: What would you like the power to do? Relationship-based ads and online behavioral advertising help us do that.</p> <p>&nbsp;</p> <p>Bank of America participates in the Digital Advertising Alliance ("DAA") self-regulatory Principles for Online Behavioral Advertising and uses the Advertising Options Icon on our behavioral ads on non-affiliated third-party sites (excluding ads appearing on platforms that do not accept the icon). Ads served on our behalf by these companies do not contain unencrypted personal information and we limit the use of personal information by companies that serve our ads. To learn more about ad choices, or to opt out of interest-based advertising with non-affiliated third-party sites, visit <a href="https://youradchoices.com/control" target="_blank" id="csp-class-67ebe147-dece-4473-b59d-ae3758ed3d0b">YourAdChoices</a> powered by the DAA or through the <a href="https://www.networkadvertising.org/choices" id="csp-class-e99e978c-66db-4f48-b031-aa1e09e6eb53">Network Advertising Initiative's Opt-Out Tool.</a> You may also visit the individual sites for additional information on their data and privacy practices and opt-out options.</p> <p>&nbsp;</p> <p>To learn more about relationship-based ads, online behavioral advertising and our privacy practices, please review the <a href="https://www.bankofamerica.com/privacy/online-privacy-notice.go" target="_blank" id="csp-class-88bce1e5-a389-4134-8204-0e1e32224b8e">Bank of America Online Privacy Notice</a> and our <a href="https://www.bankofamerica.com/privacy/faq/online-privacy-faq.go" target="_blank" id="csp-class-e29339d0-7170-40b5-b6e3-650c829b6a17">Online Privacy FAQs</a>.</p> </div> </div> <div class="footer-nav-utility__copyright"> <div class="footer-nav-utility__housing-text"><p id="csp-class-78e20ec6-3e7b-45d6-b4dd-9ba7d0de9ccc"><a id="launchOtPc" class="ot-sdk-show-settings" href="#lot">Your Privacy Choices&nbsp;</a><span aria-hidden="true" class="uc-icon-optout-policy" id="csp-class-da4bd532-7928-45f8-bf59-da1d89c67b59"></span></p> <p>Some materials and online content may be available in English only.</p> <p>&nbsp;</p> <p>Bank of America, N.A.
